# 📚 论文日报 · 翻译智能体 & Agent

本仓库每天 11:00 由本地程序自动更新：抓取 arXiv 关键词论文 + Hugging Face 本周热门论文。

表格里的「中文简介」和「相关度」由大模型根据论文摘要生成，⭐ 越多越贴近研究方向（⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ 是直接相关，⭐ 是基本无关）；点开可展开英文原摘要核对。每天另有 8 篇论文的全文深度解读，在当天 Issue 的评论里。

点右上角 **Watch** → **Custom** → 勾选 **Issues** 可以每天收到邮件通知。

关键词、数量等配置在 [`config.py`](config.py) 里修改。

Last update: 2026-11-23

We study how sparse activation, attention design, model depth and width, and speculative decoding balance model capacity with computation, memory access, and execution overhead. Our pretraining infrastructure builds on MindSpore HyperParallel, combining topology-aware hybrid sharding, efficient distributed optimizer execution, and coordinated management of long-sequence workloads and activation memory. CPU affinity and core isolation</p></details> |
| **[Jeveration: System One Models Now Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24738)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.24738) | 2026-11-18 | **149** 👍 | 该论文提出Jeveration，让System One式快速并行预测模型Jev也能单次前向生成高质量图像：在冻结的视觉语言模型上引入共享随机状态协调各位置的并行预测以保持全局一致性，并先学习离散图像码分布引导生成，还统一了文本到图像与图像到图像任务。属图像生成研究，与四个研究方向基本无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>System One models such as Jev return decisions through fast, parallel prediction. In this paper, we introduce Jeveration, showing that the same decision paradigm can also generate high-quality images in a single forward pass. Jeveration builds on a frozen vision-language model, repurposing its visual-semantic knowledge for holistic image generation. The key challenge is to maintain global consistency. A single prompt admits many valid images, yet parallel predictions must converge on a coherent whole. We therefore condition all positions on a shared random state to coordinate parallel predictions, so that each draw yields a different but coherent image. Since the model tends to ignore this state under direct image supervision, we first learn a distribution over discrete image codes to guid</p></details> |
| **[InclusionMed: Benchmarking AI for Inclusive Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21218)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21218) | 2026-11-16 | **104** 👍 | 该论文针对医学AI评测覆盖不均的问题，提出开放评测计划InclusionMed：其Atlas按三种医疗角色与三种AI能力组织基准，从388个候选中经结构化审计与独立复现筛出4个公开基准，并加入私有诊断推理基准InclusionMed-DxFiles。前沿模型总分仅17.5-49.1（满分100），显示医学AI能力缺口明显。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Advancing medical AI for inclusive healthcare requires evaluation across diverse healthcare roles, tasks, and care settings, but benchmark coverage remains uneven. With InclusionMed-DxFiles, our private diagnostic reasoning benchmark, the Atlas contains five benchmarks covering three of its nine role--capability cells. | **[Sage S2.1 Flash Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21072)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21072) | 2026-11-16 | **100** 👍 | 该论文介绍320B参数通用MoE模型Sage S1.5 Flash，面向科学证据推理与交互环境行动：用专用编码器处理分子图与生物序列并映射入共享骨干，构建工具调用、搜索、编程及机器学习实验与科学软件等可验证环境训练智能体能力，以执行结果与环境状态核验任务完成，在七个领域33个基准上评测。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>We introduce Sage S1.5 Flash, a 320-billion-parameter general-purpose mixture-of-experts model designed to reason over scientific evidence and act in interactive environments. | **[AscendGYM: A Cost-Aware Kernel Optimization Benchmark for Real-World Workloads on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17621)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.17621) | 2026-11-13 | **95** 👍 | 该论文指出Ascend NPU上现有内核基准偏重内核生成、忽视在真实负载配置下优化既有实现，遂提出AscendGYM：48个三档难度内核，测试用例的数据类型与张量形状采集自真实训练推理任务，性能提升可直接映射到实际负载；支持模型与模型-框架两种评测模式，可评估模型或自定义智能体的内核优化能力。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Kernel optimization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) training and inference on Ascend NPUs. To address this requirement, we introduce AscendGYM, a benchmark whose initial suite comprises 48 kernels across three difficulty levels. Each kernel is paired with test cases whose data types and tensor shapes are collected from real-world training and inference tasks. Consequently, performance improvements on AscendGYM map directly to the corresponding workloads. In terms of usability, two evaluation modes are supported in AscendGYM: model evaluation and model-harness evalu</p></details> |
| **[K2-Horizon: A Frontier Fleet of Open Source Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21003)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21003) | 2026-11-16 | **90** 👍 | 该论文发布K2-Horizon开源模型舰队（0.9B稠密到375B-A23B MoE），面向数学、科学、软件工程、深度研究与办公自动化等推理与智能体任务，在智能体与编程基准上领先或持平同规模开源模型；技术上提出Mixture-of-Value Attention与softplus门控，36B-A4B以七分之一激活参数接近32B稠密模型。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>We present K2-Horizon, a fleet of six fully open language models, from a 0.9B dense model to a 375B-A23B MoE, built for general-purpose reasoning and agentic work across math and science, software engineering, deep research, office automation, and beyond. The models lead or are competitive with strong open models of their size on agentic and coding benchmarks. On the modeling side, to strengthen reasoning, we introduce Mixture-of-Value Attention (MoVA), applying MoE to attention value projections, and softplus attention gating, a more flexible, theoretically motivated gate. Our 36B-A4B model uses both and nearly matches a 32B dense model trained on the same data, with one-seventh the active parameters. We also introduce Uno, lossless diffusion adapters that speed up decoding by up to 2.2x </p></details> |
| **[AllocFlow: End-to-End Portfolio Trading Action Generation from Raw Market Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19937)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.19937) | 2026-11-15 | **77** 👍 | 该论文解决量化投资中信号预测与组合构建相互解耦、学习目标与配置执行不一致的问题，提出端到端流匹配框架AllocFlow：用跨资产flow Transformer以时序市场表示为条件，从原始多资产行情联合生成带符号的连续仓位权重，并针对连续分配策略学习的样本低效与不稳定设计稳定机制。属金融方向，与用户研究方向无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Real-world portfolio management requires converting noisy market observations into coordinated, simultaneous allocations across multiple assets under shared capital constraints. Advances in forecasting models and alpha discovery help identify opportunities. However, traditional pipelines often decouple signal forecasting from portfolio construction, potentially misaligning learning with allocation and execution objectives. We present AllocFlow, an end-to-end flow-matching framework that directly generates joint portfolio allocations from raw multi-asset market histories. To capture dependencies among portfolio positions, AllocFlow employs a cross-asset flow Transformer conditioned on temporal market representations to generate continuous, signed position weights across all instruments join</p></details> |
| **[Tideway: Phase-Aware Tiered Memory Management for MoE Inference on Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14848)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.14848) | 2026-11-10 | **67** 👍 | 该论文解决消费级设备部署大MoE模型时VRAM与内存受限、权重跨VRAM/RAM/SSD换页引发性能抖动的问题，提出TIDEWAY系统：把主机RAM与GPU VRAM作为统一内存池管理，配合阶段感知的内存布局与自适应调度提升推理效率。属推理系统优化工作，与翻译、生物、教学智能体均无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly becoming a dominant paradigm for AI applications. Recently, there is a growing demand to deploy large-scale Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models locally on consumer PCs to balance data privacy, API costs, and inference efficiency. However, unlike datacenter servers with abundant DRAM, consumer devices are tightly constrained in both GPU VRAM and host RAM, forcing massive MoE weights to spill across VRAM, host RAM, and SSD. Under such spilling, we observe that existing systems fail to fully utilize this memory hierarchy, treating the tiers as independent caches and thrashing in inference. We present TIDEWAY, a system for efficient inference of large MoE models on memory-constrained consumer devices. Our core insight is to manage host RAM and GPU VRAM</p></details> |
| **[Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22055)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22055) | 2026-11-17 | **59** 👍 | 该论文探讨权重空间编程是否还需Transformer：让三个前沿LLM仅凭10个标注样例和沙盒，手写固定架构的权重或自行设计架构。结果三者都把明文事实编译成稀疏表并用固定过程查询，最高达54.4%测试准确率，比同一LLM写出的最优Transformer少约1800倍参数且更准。与四个研究方向无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Standard neural architectures such as the Transformer were chosen because they train well with gradient descent and run fast on GPUs. In weight-space programming, a large language model (LLM) writes target weights by hand without gradient-based training, so those reasons no longer apply. Should we keep the Transformer? Given minimal guidance, ten labeled examples, and a sandbox, mainly on multiple-choice questions, we task three frontier LLMs with either generating the weights of a fixed architecture or designing the architecture themselves. When free to design, all three compile plain-text facts into a sparse table queried by a fixed procedure, reaching up to 54.4% test accuracy. An LLM-designed model achieves higher test accuracy than the most accurate released Transformer that the same </p></details> |
| **[WeVisForensics: Self-Evolving Agent for Omni AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21373)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21373) | 2026-11-16 | **56** 👍 | 该论文针对AI生成视频检测中单次思维链易漏短暂局部伪影、预定义多智能体流程灵活性不足的问题，提出WeVisForensics：主调查智能体调度可自进化的视觉与音频取证子智能体库，学习该查哪种伪影、哪个时间窗、何时结束；双时间尺度策略优化DTPO让调查策略与取证专家共同进化。属检测领域的智能体框架。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Recent advances in video generation call for reliable detection grounded in explicit forensic evidence. However, current methods primarily depend on single-pass chain-of-thought reasoning that overlooks brief or localized artifacts, or on predefined multi-agent workflows that offer limited flexibility in selecting relevant inspections. Even with adaptive investigation, optimizing the investigation policy alone does not directly improve the forensic capabilities of fixed experts. We introduce WeVisForensics, an agentic framework in which a main investigator agent coordinates a self-evolving library of forensic inspector sub-agents spanning visual and audio cues. The investigator learns which artifact type to examine, over which window, and when to conclude, and these interactions provide sh</p></details> |
| **[Mind2Act World: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Embodied Mind, Physical Action, and Mind-Action Composition in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26444)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26444) | 2026-11-19 | **53** 👍 | 该论文针对具身智能评测无法区分失败源于推理、执行还是二者组合的问题，提出Mind2Act World诊断基准：来自五个真实应用域的30个仿真操作任务，各三档难度、两种场景共180种配置，分别评测Mind（基于历史的决策）、Act（精确及时执行）及两者组合能力。属机器人具身方向，与用户研究方向无关。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Embodied intelligence aims to let robots perform complex physical tasks with human-like adaptability and reliability, which requires three complementary capabilities: history-based cognitive reasoning, precise and timely physical execution, and the composition of reasoning and execution through environmental feedback. Yet existing benchmarks either evaluate reasoning and execution within the same tasks or assess them separately without their composition, so they reveal little about whether a failure comes from reasoning, execution, or their composition. | **[Linear-time Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding with Reference Aggregation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04251v3)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2402.04251v3) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>ACL 2024. v3: More accurate time measurements for ChrF</p></details> | 2026-11-10 | 该论文针对 Minimum Bayes Risk 解码需对大量采样序列两两计算效用指标、复杂度为 O(n²) 的开销问题，提出用聚合参考表示近似成对得分，将效用估计复杂度降至 O(n)，同时经验上保留 MBR 解码的大部分翻译质量增益，并开源了代码。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding is a text generation technique that has been shown to improve the quality of machine translations, but is expensive, even if a sampling-based approximation is used. | **[Doc2FRC: Length-Consistent Document-Level Machine Translation via Fixed-Range Chunking](http://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12674v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.12674v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>To appear in Proceedings of the Eleventh Conference on Machine Translation (WMT2026)</p></details> | 2026-09-11 | 该论文针对长上下文 LLM 直接做 Doc2Doc 翻译易出现 n-gram 重复与质量退化、规则分块又造成训练与推理长度分布失配的问题，提出 Fixed-Range Chunking，用动态规划把文档切分到预定义长度区间并统一用于训练和推理，辅以轻量双边界匹配算法。显著缓解长度失配并提升文档级翻译质量。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Advanced large language models (LLMs) with long context windows can substantially reduce input truncation in document-level machine translation (DocMT). Nonetheless, conventional rule-based chunking approaches fail to handle the length distribution mismatch between training and inference. To address this, we introduce Fixed-Range Chunking (FRC), utilizing dynamic programming to partition documents into chunks within a predefined length interval. By consistently applying FRC during training and inference, the input documents of any length are mapped to the same length distribution, substantially reducing train-test </p></details> |
| **[The Blindness of Document-Level Translation Evaluation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05949v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.05949v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Accepted to EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference)</p></details> | 2026-09-05 | 该论文质疑文档级 MT 评测协议的有效性：设计 MIX 反事实条件，将来自不同系统的句段拼成文档，在 18,420 条英韩专家标注与 14 个自动指标上发现分数、系统排名与错误标注在连贯与不连贯文档间统计等价。说明标注者并未真正做出文档级判断，问题出在协议而非标注者。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Document-level machine translation (MT) evaluation extends segment-level protocols by presenting full documents to annotators, on the assumption that such presentation elicits document-level judgments. | **[Enhancing Document-Level Machine Translation via Filtered Synthetic Corpora and Two-Stage LLM Adaptation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22186v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.22186v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Accepted to ICASSP 2026</p></details> | 2026-03-23 | 该论文针对 LLM 做文档级翻译时高质量平行数据稀缺且易产生幻觉遗漏的问题，先用 LLM 将摘要数据改写为文档级平行数据，再用 sacreBLEU、COMET 与 LaBSE 余弦相似度等多指标过滤，之后进行两阶段微调。实验显示该流程有效提升 LLM 文档级翻译质量。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>In Machine Translation, Large Language Models (LLMs) have generally underperformed compared to conventional encoder-decoder systems and thus see limited adoption. | **[Pralekha: Cross-Lingual Document Alignment for Indic Languages](http://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19096v3)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2411.19096v3) | 2025-11-10 | 该论文针对印地语系语言跨语言文档对齐中元数据稀缺、句级对齐搜索空间组合爆炸的问题，发布含 300 余万对齐文档对、覆盖 11 种印度语言与英语的基准 Pralekha，并提出细粒度对齐指标 Document Alignment Coefficient (DAC)。为文档级 MT 的数据挖掘提供了资源与方法。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Mining parallel document pairs for document-level machine translation (MT) remains challenging due to the limitations of existing Cross-Lingual Document Alignment (CLDA) techniques. | **[DocHPLT: A Massively Multilingual Document-Level Translation Dataset](http://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13079v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.13079v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>WMT 2025</p></details> | 2025-09-29 | 该论文解决文档级翻译资源集中于少数高资源语言的问题，构建迄今最大的公开文档级翻译数据集 DocHPLT：50 种语言与英语配对、1.24 亿对齐文档对、共 42.6 亿句，通过改造网页抽取管线保留文档完整结构而非拼接句子级数据。为文档级翻译训练、评测与长上下文建模提供基础。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Existing document-level machine translation resources are only available for a handful of languages, mostly high-resourced ones. | **[Align-then-Slide: A complete evaluation framework for Ultra-Long Document-Level Machine Translation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03809v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.03809v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>under preview</p></details> | 2025-09-04 | 该论文针对 LLM 整篇输出破坏句级对齐评测假设的问题，提出 Align-then-Slide 框架：先自动推断句级源目标对应并重建目标端以解决漏译与多对一映射，再以 1 至 4 chunk 滑动方式计算多粒度指标分。在 WMT 基准上与专家 MQM 排名的 Pearson 相关达 0.929，与人工判断高度一致。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large language models (LLMs) have ushered in a new era for document-level machine translation (\textit{doc}-mt), yet their whole-document outputs challenge existing evaluation methods that assume sentence-by-sentence alignment. | **[Multilingual Contextualization of Large Language Models for Document-Level Machine Translation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12140v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2504.12140v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>COLM 2025</p></details> | 2025-08-28 | 该论文针对 LLM 从句子级扩展到文档级翻译时长依赖与跨句语篇现象建模不足的问题，构建高质量文档级数据集 DocBlocks 并做针对性微调，整合带与不带上下文指令的 Doc2Doc 与分块翻译等多种范式。实验表明多范式结合提升文档级翻译并保持句子级性能。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in sentence-level machine translation, but scaling to document-level translation remains challenging, particularly in modeling long-range dependencies and discourse phenomena across sentences and paragraphs. | **[Investigating Length Issues in Document-level Machine Translation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17592v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2412.17592v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Accepted at the MT Summit 2025</p></details> | 2025-04-28 | 该工作精确测量输入长度对文档级机器翻译的影响，实验表明翻译性能随输入变长而下降，且文档靠前的句子译文质量更高；调整文档长度分布与位置编码仅能边际缓解，说明即使长上下文 Transformer 也难以真正处理数千 token 的整篇文档。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Transformer architectures are increasingly effective at processing and generating very long chunks of texts, opening new perspectives for document-level machine translation (MT). | **[Fine-Grained and Multi-Dimensional Metrics for Document-Level Machine Translation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20941v4)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2410.20941v4) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Accepted at NAACL 2025 Student Research Workshop</p></details> | 2025-04-20 | 论文评估指令微调 LLM 单次直接翻译整篇文档的能力，发现其优于逐句翻译，但 BLEU 偏向句级译文而无法体现该优势；进而提出 LLM-as-a-judge 评测范式，用 GPT-4 从连贯性、准确性、流畅度等维度细粒度评估文档级翻译。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large language models (LLMs) have excelled in various NLP tasks, including machine translation (MT), yet most studies focus on sentence-level translation. | **[Improving LLM-based Document-level Machine Translation with Multi-Knowledge Fusion](http://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12152v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.12152v1) | 2025-03-15 | 该论文指出 LLM 文档级翻译仅依赖展平句子序列这一单一知识源的局限，提出多知识融合方法：先用 LLM 生成文档摘要与实体翻译作为补充知识，再融合生成最终译文。实验表明多源知识能显著增强 LLM 的文档级翻译表现。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Recent studies in prompting large language model (LLM) for document-level machine translation (DMT) primarily focus on the inter-sentence context by flatting the source document into a long sequence. | **[Source-primed Multi-turn Conversation Helps Large Language Models Translate Documents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10494v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.10494v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>9 pages, 2 figures</p></details> | 2025-03-13 | 论文提出多轮对话式文档翻译：把文档分段并迭代翻译、保留历史轮次以保证连贯，无需训练且可复用前轮 KV cache 降低开销；source-primed 变体先输入完整源文档再逐段翻译。多项自动指标显示优于整篇单轮翻译和分段独立翻译。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>LLMs have paved the way for truly simple document-level machine translation, but challenges such as omission errors remain. | **[DOLFIN -- Document-Level Financial test set for Machine Translation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03053v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.03053v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>To be published in NAACL 2025 Findings</p></details> | 2025-02-05 | 针对文档级 MT 测试集稀缺、集中于通用领域、仍沿用句级对齐逻辑的问题，该工作构建金融领域测试集 DOLFIN：以章节而非句子为对齐单元，支持信息重组等真篇章现象，覆盖 5 个语言对、平均 1950 个对齐章节，并给出详细数据构建流程。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Despite the strong research interest in document-level Machine Translation (MT), the test sets dedicated to this task are still scarce. | **[M3T: A New Benchmark Dataset for Multi-Modal Document-Level Machine Translation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08255v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2406.08255v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>NAACL 2024, dataset at https://github.com/amazon-science/m3t-multi-modal-translation-bench</p></details> | 2024-06-12 | 真实 PDF 文档版式复杂，OCR 或规则提取文本易错，且段落、标题等布局隐含远距离文本关联，被现有 NMT 系统忽视。该论文提出多模态文档级翻译基准 M3T，以视觉形式呈现的真实文档直接评估翻译系统，填补富结构文档翻译评测的空白。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Document translation poses a challenge for Neural Machine Translation (NMT) systems. CAP takes into account multi-level attention, selects the most relevant sentences to the current one as context, and then generates a summary from these collected sentences. Subsequently, sentences most similar t</p></details> |

| **[AppSci-Bench: Can LLMs Build AI Agents and Make Them Better?](http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25360v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25360v1) | 2026-11-19 | 该论文提出 AppSci-Bench，评测 LLM 能否像人类应用科学家一样构建并改进 Agent：向利益相关者获取需求、探索业务基础设施、设计实验和内部基准来优化目标 Agent，覆盖 Slides Generation、合规审查、航空等七个真实领域，并对七个前沿模型进行系统评估。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used not only to power AI agents but also to build them. AppSci-Bench features seven realistic domains - five new ones (Slides Generation, Order-to-Cash, Compliance Review, Customer Call Insights, and CRM Insights) and two domains recast from tau3-bench (Yao et al., 2024; Shi et al., 2026b)(Airline and Banking) </p></details> |
| **[Building Presentation Intelligence for High-Quality Slide Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30888v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30888v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>28 pages</p></details> | 2026-10-22 | 该论文面向高质量自动 PPT 生成提出统一训练框架：构建显式控制难度与有效性的交叉验证数据合成流水线，用规则与先验知识增强教师模型的指导能力，并结合可验证奖励优化，系统提升模型演示智能水平。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>PowerPoint presentations (PPTs) are a dominant medium for communication across diverse domains, yet high-quality PPT generation still depends heavily on labor-intensive human effort. In this work, we present a unified training framework for automatic PPT generation toward presentation intelligence, integrating cross-validated data synthesis, guided teacher enhancement, and verifiable reward optimization. We first construct a cross-validated pipeline for data synthesis, with explicit control over both difficulty and validity. We then develop a teacher-enhancement framework grounded in rules and prior knowledge to substantially</p></details> |
| **[InfoVis: Unified Slide Image Generation and Editing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16784v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16784v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Code: https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/InfoVis</p></details> | 2026-10-12 | 该论文将幻灯片生成与编辑统一为四个任务，构建含 1300 余万条持续预训练与 190 万条微调记录的数据引擎及人工筛选基准 VisSlideBench（1527 例），训练出 9B 统一模型 InfoVis，在全部 11 项指标上领先各开源模型。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Generating and editing presentation slides requires models to combine accurate text rendering, instruction following, visual grounding, aesthetic design, and precise editing within one canvas. Existing work covers only subsets of these capabilities and lacks large-scale slide-native supervision and diagnostic evaluation. We also introduce VisSlideBench, a benchmark of 1,527 manually screened instances that covers all four tasks with task-specific, multidimensional evaluation. Its automatic rankings agree with blinded, dimension-wise human rankings. Using these resources, we train InfoVis, a unified 9B slide generation and editing model.</p></details> |
| **[SlideLab: Audience-Centered Scientific Slide Generation and Evaluation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30294v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.30294v2) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>Update Appendix and add few more ablations, ConfArena architecture</p></details> | 2026-10-08 | SlideLab 是免训练多智能体框架，从研究论文生成科学演示：先规划叙事，再由内容规划、视觉生成、布局修正、溯源验证等智能体迭代完善幻灯片。盲测人类偏好中获 77% 胜率且 token 消耗约为最强开源基线的四分之一；另提出观众视角评测框架 ConfArena。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Scientific presentations are more than summaries of research papers. They need to present the work in a coherent sequence, explain the main ideas clearly, and help the audience follow the presentation. We present SlideLab, a training-free multi-agent framework for generating scientific presentations from research papers. SlideLab first plans the presentation narrative, then builds and iteratively refines a shared slide deck using agents for content planning, visual generation, layout refinement, and grounding verification. In a blind human preference study, SlideLab was preferred over both open-source and commercial systems on 77% of papers while using roughly 4 times fewer inference tokens than the strongest open-source baseline. We also introduce ConfArena, an audience-oriented evaluatio</p></details> |
| **[ReDeck: Step-Level Render-Grounded Refinement for Document-to-Slide Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00194v2)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00194v2) | 2026-09-05 | 针对文档转幻灯片中'一轮一反馈'导致溢出、重叠、裁剪等局部错误难定位修复的问题，ReDeck 将修订拆为原子编辑动作，每步编辑即返回渲染器观测，实现'一编辑一观测'，并结合步骤级渲染反馈与回合级自适应 critic 的多粒度反馈机制。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Document-to-slide generation is challenging because slides are dense editable artifacts that require both faithful content selection and precise spatial layout. This delayed feedback makes local failures such as overflow, overlap, clipping, and off-canvas placement difficult to attribute and repair. | **[Decision-Centric Research: A Calibrated Decision Model and Benchmark for Scientific Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00774v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.00774v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>11 pages, 3 figures, 9 tables</p></details> | 2026-10-31 | 科学 agent 的选择埋在生成文本里难以独立评估；DCR 将单个决策作为监督与评测单元，在显式 Research State 上做类型化预测，定义 24 种决策类型并构建 2000 例 RD-Bench；0.6B 的 DCRM 状态感知版 Top-1 达 89.4%，远超 question-only 版与 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Scientific agents make consequential choices about evidence, next actions and research strategy, but choices embedded in generated text are difficult to evaluate independently. | **[Epistemic Guard: Out-of-Process MCP Receipt Proxying, Post-Turn Prose Supervision, and Provenance-Gated Memory for Tool-Using Autonomous Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27095v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27095v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>8 pages, 3 tables; open-source code and osaudit-verified manifests at https://github.com/AlienatedAliens/epistemic-guard (v0.5.2)</p></details> | 2026-11-20 | 针对MCP工具调用智能体的三类运行时验证缺口——编造动作、来源坍缩与同源回声放大,提出开源库epistemic-guard,以进程外Ed25519签名的收据代理记录工具执行凭证,并在CPU上校验模型事后叙述与真实执行是否一致,无需GPU显存即可将prose与receipt绑定。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Language-model agents using the Model Context Protocol (MCP) face three runtime verification gaps: (i) action fabrication, where post-tool prose narrates side effects that failed, timed out, or never ran; (ii) provenance collapse, where unverified model prose or web text enters durable memory alongside executed tool outputs; and (iii) syndicated echo amplification, where mirrored web pages satisfy multi-source corroboration from one origin. Existing MCP firewalls inspect wire calls before execution, while LLM-judge guardrails require multi-gigabyte VRAM without binding prose to receipts. | **[$R^2$: Recognizing and Reducing Redundant Visual Tool Use](http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27070v1)** [📄](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27070v1) <details><summary>💬</summary><p>18 pages, 7 figures, including appendices</p></details> | 2026-11-20 | 研究视觉工具调用何时有益、何时冗余:提出样本级CAT分析按模型能否获益对样本分类,发现七个benchmark中真正受益于工具的样本至多占12.44%;并提出Visual Feedback Dependency指标评估单次调用,揭示基准分数可能掩盖模型真实的工具使用能力。<br><br>**相关度 ⭐⭐⭐**<br><br><details><summary>📄 英文原摘要</summary><p>Recent studies have shown that visual tools can improve multimodal reasoning. We present $R^2$, a framework that recognizes and reduces redundant visual tool use in vision-language models. We first introduce a sample-level Category Analysis of Tool Utility (CAT), which categorizes samples according to whether a model can benefit from tools. This analysis reveals that benchmark scores may obscure a model's tool-use ability. Across seven benchmarks, samples benefiting from tools account for at most 12.44% of each benchmark, while the majority gain little from tool interaction or are even harmed by it. We therefore study agent harness distillation: using a domain- or instance-optimized harness as training-time guidance and transferring the behaviors it induces into model weights, so that its gains survive under a single fixed target harness.
